U04.S182Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a9e82df0296f38f71/tests/contracts/test_doubles.py (174 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-a9e82df0296f38f71/tests/contracts/test_doubles.py
"""Each Protocol's replay double serves its recording."""

import importlib
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest

import rllab.core as core

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))
contract_io = importlib.import_module("contract_io")

RNG = np.random.default_rng(0)


def world():
    return contract_io.module("core.World").load()


def recorded(symbol, case):
    return contract_io.raw(symbol, case)


def test_world_double_replays_its_recording():
    w, data = world(), recorded("core.World", "seed42_snake")
    first = w.reset(42)
    assert first == contract_io.decode(core.Step, data["steps"][0])
    assert first.obs.shape == (len(w.spec.agents), w.spec.obs_len)
    assert first.obs.dtype == np.float32
    for i, action in enumerate(data["actions"], start=1):
        step = w.step(np.array(action))
        assert step == contract_io.decode(core.Step, data["steps"][i])
        assert w.draw() == contract_io.decode(core.DrawState, data["draws"][i])
    assert step.done
    assert step.truncated
    assert w.result() == contract_io.decode(core.EpisodeResult, data["result"])


def test_world_double_refuses_bad_actions_and_steps_after_the_end():
    w = world()
    w.reset(42)
    with pytest.raises(core.ActionError) as caught:
        w.step(np.array([3]))
    assert (caught.value.agent, caught.value.value) == ("snake", [3])
    for _ in range(5):
        w.step(np.array([2]))
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        w.step(np.array([2]))


def test_policy_double_plays_the_world_doubles_episode():
    w, policy = world(), contract_io.module("core.Policy").load()
    step, actions = w.reset(42), []
    while not step.done:
        action = policy.act(step.obs, RNG)
        actions.append(action.tolist())
        step = w.step(action)
    assert actions == recorded("core.World", "seed42_snake")["actions"]
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        policy.act(np.zeros((1, 12), np.float32), RNG)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", ["corridor", "target_point"])
def test_check_world_double_serves_states_and_best_actions(case):
    double = contract_io.module("worlds.CheckWorld").load(case)
    data = recorded("worlds.CheckWorld", case)
    states, best = double.states(), double.best_actions()
    assert states.shape == (6, double.spec.obs_len)
    assert len(best) == len(states)
    assert np.array_equal(best, np.asarray(data["best_actions"]))
    step = double.reset(0)
    for action in data["actions"]:
        step = double.step(np.array(action))
    assert step.done
    assert double.result().length == len(data["actions"])


def test_corridor_best_actions_are_inside_discrete_2():
    best = contract_io.module("worlds.CheckWorld").load("corridor").best_actions()
    assert set(best.tolist()) <= {0, 1}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", ["snake_5", "fish_5"])
def test_random_policy_double_acts_inside_its_action_space(case):
    double = contract_io.module("baselines.RandomPolicy").load(case)
    actions = [double.act(np.zeros((1, 12), np.float32), RNG) for _ in range(5)]
    space = double.action
    for action in actions:
        if isinstance(space, core.Discrete):
            assert action.shape == (1,)
            assert 0 <= action[0] < space.n
        else:
            assert action.shape == (1, space.dims)
            assert np.all((action >= space.low) & (action <= space.high))


def test_rule_policy_doubles_describe_themselves_in_one_sentence():
    module = contract_io.module("baselines.RulePolicy")
    for case in contract_io.cases("baselines.RulePolicy"):
        rule = module.load(case)
        assert rule.description.endswith(".")
        assert rule.description.count(". ") == 0
        obs, action = rule.pairs[0]
        assert np.array_equal(rule.act(obs, RNG), action)


def test_snake_rule_double_matches_the_policy_double():
    rule = contract_io.module("baselines.RulePolicy").load("snake")
    policy = contract_io.module("core.Policy").load()
    for (obs, action), (obs2, action2) in zip(rule.pairs, policy.pairs, strict=True):
        assert np.array_equal(obs, obs2)
        assert np.array_equal(action, action2)


def test_learner_double_learns_over_the_world_doubles_episodes():
    learner = contract_io.module("methods.Learner").load()
    w = world()
    for _ in range(2):
        step = w.reset(42)
        while not step.done:
            action = learner.act(step.obs, RNG)
            step = w.step(action)
    assert len(learner.transitions) == 10
    for t in learner.transitions:
        learner.observe(t)
    assert learner.observed == learner.transitions
    greedy = learner.policy()
    assert greedy.act(w.reset(42).obs, RNG).shape == (1,)
    state = learner.state_dict()
    state["steps"] = -1
    assert learner.state_dict()["steps"] == 10


def test_learner_double_transitions_have_one_row_per_team_agent():
    learner = contract_io.module("methods.Learner").load()
    for t in learner.transitions:
        assert t.obs.shape == t.next_obs.shape == (1, 12)
        assert t.actions.shape == t.rewards.shape == t.active.shape == (1,)


def test_run_double_serves_a_finished_run():
    run = contract_io.module("runlog.Run").load()
    assert run.record.status == "finished"
    budget = run.record.config.budget
    assert [p.step for p in run.evals()] == list(range(0, budget + 1, budget // 20))
    assert [c.pct for c in run.checkpoints()] == [0, 50, 100]
    assert all(c.spec == run.record.spec for c in run.checkpoints())
    assert all(set(e.scores) == {"snake"} for e in run.episodes())


def test_renamed_run_double_moves_its_record_and_checkpoints():
    module = contract_io.module("runlog.Run")
    run = module.renamed(module.load(), "snake-s6", 6, "incomplete")
    assert (run.record.config.name, run.record.config.seed) == ("snake-s6", 6)
    assert run.record.ended is None
    assert all(c.path.parent.parent.name == "snake-s6" for c in run.checkpoints())


def test_run_writer_double_records_the_writes(tmp_path):
    module = contract_io.module("runlog.RunWriter")
    writer = module.writer(tmp_path)
    record = module.replay_into(writer)
    assert record.status == "finished"
    assert record.path == tmp_path / "snake" / "default" / "snake-s1"
    assert contract_io.same_json(writer.calls, recorded("runlog.RunWriter", "snake_3_checkpoints")["calls"])


def test_run_writer_double_refuses_an_existing_run(tmp_path):
    module = contract_io.module("runlog.RunWriter")
    (tmp_path / "snake" / "default" / "snake-s1").mkdir(parents=True)
    with pytest.raises(FileExistsError):
        module.writer(tmp_path)

pytest.raises(FileExistsError):\n        module.writer(tmp_path)\n"

"""Each boundary function's fake serves its fixtures, with the contract's signature."""

import importlib
import inspect
import json
import pickle
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest
import torch

import rllab.baselines
import rllab.charts
import rllab.core
import rllab.evaluate
import rllab.methods
import rllab.runlog
import rllab.train
import rllab.worlds

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))
contract_io = importlib.import_module("contract_io")

RNG = np.random.default_rng(0)
FAKES = {
    "core.play_episode": rllab.core.play_episode,
    "worlds.make": rllab.worlds.make,
    "baselines.rule_for": rllab.baselines.rule_for,
    "baselines.baseline_means": rllab.baselines.baseline_means,
    "methods.make_learner": rllab.methods.make_learner,
    "methods.load_policy": rllab.methods.load_policy,
    "train.load_run_configs": rllab.train.load_run_configs,
    "train.run_check": rllab.train.run_check,
    "train.run_train": rllab.train.run_train,
    "train.run_batch": rllab.train.run_batch,
    "runlog.find_runs": rllab.runlog.find_runs,
    "runlog.open_run": rllab.runlog.open_run,
    "runlog.progress": rllab.runlog.progress,
    "charts.learning_curve": rllab.charts.learning_curve,
    "evaluate.final_evaluation": rllab.evaluate.final_evaluation,
}


def fake(symbol):
    return getattr(contract_io.module(symbol), symbol.split(".")[1])


def shape(fn):
    return [(p.name, p.kind, p.default) for p in inspect.signature(fn).parameters.values()]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("symbol", sorted(FAKES))
def test_fake_has_the_contract_signature(symbol):
    assert shape(fake(symbol)) == shape(FAKES[symbol])


def test_make_fake_serves_the_snake_double():
    make = fake("worlds.make")
    world = make("snake")
    assert world.spec.world == "snake"
    assert world.reset(42).obs.shape == (1, world.spec.obs_len)
    for bad in (lambda: make("chess"), lambda: make("snake", "big"), lambda: make("snake", grid=5)):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=r"chess|big|grid"):
            bad()


def test_play_episode_fake_serves_the_traced_seed_7_episode():
    play = fake("core.play_episode")
    world = contract_io.module("core.World").load()
    policies = {"snake": contract_io.module("core.Policy").load()}
    traced = play(world, policies, 7, trace=True)
    assert traced.result.seed == 7
    assert len(traced.frames) == traced.result.length + 1
    assert play(world, policies, 7).frames is None
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="snake"):
        play(world, {}, 7)


def test_rule_for_fake_serves_the_snake_rule():
    rule_for = fake("baselines.rule_for")
    rule = rule_for("snake", "default", "snake")
    assert "food" in rule.description
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="prey"):
        rule_for("prey", "default", "prey")


def test_baseline_means_fake_serves_the_snake_means():
    baseline_means = fake("baselines.baseline_means")
    assert baseline_means("snake", "default") == contract_io.sample("baselines.BaselineMeans", "snake")
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        baseline_means("fish", "one")


def test_make_learner_fake_serves_dqn_and_refuses_a_mismatch():
    make_learner = fake("methods.make_learner")
    dqn = contract_io.sample("core.MethodConfig", "snake_dqn_defaults")
    ppo = contract_io.sample("core.MethodConfig", "ppo_lr_override")
    snake = contract_io.sample("core.WorldSpec", "snake_default")
    fish = contract_io.sample("core.WorldSpec", "fish_local")
    learner = make_learner(dqn, snake, "snake", RNG)
    assert learner.state_dict()["steps"] == 10
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="dqn"):
        make_learner(dqn, fish, "fish", RNG)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="ppo"):
        make_learner(ppo, snake, "snake", RNG)


def test_load_policy_fake_serves_the_snake_rule_and_names_spec_mismatches():
    load_policy = fake("methods.load_policy")
    ckpt = contract_io.sample("runlog.Checkpoint", "snake_100")
    policy = load_policy(ckpt, "snake", ckpt.spec)
    obs = contract_io.module("core.World").load().reset(42).obs
    assert policy.act(obs, RNG).shape == (1,)
    with pytest.raises(rllab.methods.SpecMismatchError) as caught:
        load_policy(ckpt, "snake", contract_io.sample("core.WorldSpec", "fish_local"))
    assert caught.value.fields == ("obs_len", "action")


def test_load_run_configs_fake_serves_two_snake_seeds():
    load = fake("train.load_run_configs")
    configs = load(Path("configs/snake.toml"), [1, 2])
    assert [(c.name, c.seed) for c in configs] == [("snake-s1", 1), ("snake-s2", 2)]
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
        load(Path("configs/none.toml"), [1])


def test_run_check_fake_serves_the_passing_dqn_check():
    (result,) = fake("train.run_check")(Path("configs/snake.toml"))
    assert (result.method, result.passed) == ("dqn", True)


def test_run_train_fake_writes_a_finished_run_folder(tmp_path):
    run_train = fake("train.run_train")
    config = contract_io.sample("core.RunConfig", "snake")
    record = run_train(config, tmp_path)
    folder = tmp_path / "snake" / "default" / "snake-s1"
    assert record.path == folder
    assert record.status == "finished"
    saved = contract_io.decode(rllab.runlog.RunRecord, json.loads((folder / "run.json").read_text()))
    assert saved == record
    evals = (folder / "evals.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()
    assert json.loads(evals[-1])["step"] == config.budget
    ckpt = torch.load(folder / "checkpoints" / "ckpt_100.pt", weights_only=True)
    assert contract_io.decode(rllab.runlog.Checkpoint, ckpt).pct == 100
    with pytest.raises(rllab.runlog.RunExistsError):
        run_train(config, tmp_path)


def test_run_train_fake_pickles_by_reference_for_spawn():
    run_train = fake("train.run_train")
    assert pickle.loads(pickle.dumps(run_train)) is run_train


def test_run_batch_fake_serves_two_finished_records(tmp_path):
    records = fake("train.run_batch")(Path("configs/snake.toml"), [1, 2], tmp_path)
    assert [r.config.name for r in records] == ["snake-s1", "snake-s2"]
    assert all(r.status == "finished" and r.path.parent == tmp_path / "snake" / "default" for r in records)


def test_find_runs_fake_serves_finished_runs_plus_named_ones():
    find_runs = fake("runlog.find_runs")
    runs = find_runs(Path("runs"), "snake", "default")
    assert [r.record.config.name for r in runs] == [f"snake-s{i}" for i in range(1, 6)]
    with_incomplete = find_runs(Path("runs"), "snake", "default", include=["snake-s6"])
    assert with_incomplete[-1].record.status == "incomplete"
    assert find_runs(Path("runs"), "fish", "one") == []


def test_open_run_fake_serves_the_finished_run():
    open_run = fake("runlog.open_run")
    assert open_run(Path("runs/snake/default/snake-s1")).record.status == "finished"
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
        open_run(Path("runs/snake/default/missing"))


def test_progress_fake_serves_the_35_percent_snapshot():
    progress = fake("runlog.progress")
    snapshot = progress(Path("runs/snake/default/snake-s1"))
    assert (snapshot.step, snapshot.budget, snapshot.latest.step) == (700, 2000, 700)
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
        progress(Path("runs/none"))


def test_learning_curve_fake_writes_a_png_chart(tmp_path):
    runs = fake("runlog.find_runs")(Path("runs"), "snake", "default")
    means = contract_io.sample("baselines.BaselineMeans", "snake")
    output = fake("charts.learning_curve")(runs, means, tmp_path)
    assert output.kind == "chart"
    assert output.path.read_bytes().startswith(b"\x89PNG")
    assert output.made_from == tuple(f"snake-s{i}" for i in range(1, 6))


def test_final_evaluation_fake_serves_the_passing_summary(tmp_path):
    runs = fake("runlog.find_runs")(Path("runs"), "snake", "default")
    (summary,) = fake("evaluate.final_evaluation")(runs, tmp_path)
    assert summary.verdict.beats_baselines
    assert summary.runs == tuple(r.record.config.name for r in runs)

GATE FAILED: scripts/check.sh gate --pkg rllab. I couldn't fix this from inside the task, and nothing in my code causes it. `dt claim` wrote `rllab` to `.claude/.check_pkg`, and the stop hook passes that on as `--pkg rllab`. But `pkg_scope` in `scripts/check.sh` lists only the components (`core`, `worlds`, …, `cli`), not `rllab`, so `check.sh` exits with "unknown package" before running any check. Both files are tooling: `scripts/` is protected and the claim file is outside my Writes, and none of the escalation types fits. Plain `scripts/check.sh gate` passes in full (ruff, mypy, size, deptry, import-linter, 214 contract tests, floor_guard, protected paths), and pyright is clean too. To fix it, build-setup could add `rllab` to `pkg_scope`, or `dt claim` could skip writing `.check_pkg` for a single-package project.
